AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='Fixed. The trip was colliding with **itself** during the overlap validation.\n\n## Root cause\n\n`ManageTripFragment` builds its edit copy with `new Trip((Trip) oldObject, true)` — `keepId=true`, so the in-memory trip carries the **same UUID as its own saved database row**.\n\nOn Save, `verifyUserInput()` called `Logbook.tripExists(getNewTrip())`, which loops over *every* trip in the logbook — including that saved row — and tests `Trip.overlapsTrip()`:\n\n```java\nreturn trip.getStartDate().before(mEndDate) && trip.getEndDate().after(mStartDate);\n```\n\nCompared against itself, this is `start < end && end > start` — **true for any trip with a non-zero date range**. So every date edit was rejected with `error_trip_date`.\n\nThe `mDateChanged || !isEditing()` guard was a partial workaround: it suppressed the self-collision for edits that *didn\'t* touch dates, which is precisely why the bug only surfaced on the "modify date → save" path.\n\n## Fix\n\n**`Logbook.java`** — added an overload that can omit one trip from the search:\n\n```java\npublic static boolean tripExists(Trip trip) {\n    return tripExists(trip, null);   // original behaviour preserved\n}\n\npublic static boolean tripExists(Trip trip, UUID excludeId) {\n    for (UserDefineObject object : getTrips()) {\n        if (excludeId != null && excludeId.equals(object.getId()))\n            continue;\n        if (trip.overlapsTrip((Trip) object))\n            return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n```\n\n**`ManageTripFragment.java`** — excludes the trip being edited:\n\n```java\nUUID excludeId = isEditing() ? getEditingId() : null;\nif ((mDateChanged || !isEditing()) && Logbook.tripExists(getNewTrip(), excludeId)) {\n```\n\n## Notes on scope\n\n- I kept the single-arg `tripExists(Trip)` semantics intact deliberately — `LogbookTest:472` asserts `tripExists(trip1)` is `true` for a trip already in the logbook, which relies on the self-match. Changing `overlapsTrip()` itself (e.g. short-circuiting on equal IDs) would have broken that test and the add-trip path.\n- I left the `mDateChanged` guard in place rather than removing it as now-redundant, so edits that don\'t touch dates keep their existing behaviour on pre-existing overlapping data.\n\n## Verification\n\n- `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.\n- Since the overlap logic lives in `androidTest` (needs an emulator), I extracted `overlapsTrip`/`tripExists` verbatim into a standalone harness and confirmed: the repro now passes (`true` → `false`), while a genuinely overlapping *different* trip is still rejected, a non-overlapping trip is still accepted, editing a trip into a slot occupied by another trip is still rejected, and the existing `LogbookTest` assertion still holds.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 883, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 54988, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 883, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 2, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAPraPHGqXtnKvbKqkT4', stop_reason=None, session_id='179d3361-8d7e-4528-aff6-6d911b64db94', uuid='310a1168-5a4a-49f4-bd03-7b2adb4160f6')
